---
title: "Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)"
description: "Opt-in --depth-mode=expansive on deep-research seeds depth-one frontier nodes from the topic, admits proposals under caps, and keeps a convergence stop open while frontier nodes stay open."
trigger_phrases:

  - "expansive depth research"

# Expansive depth mode (--depth-mode=expansive)

## 1. OVERVIEW

`--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A run without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` turns on a research frontier: the run seeds depth-one nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as nodes under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps, and holds a convergence stop while frontier nodes stay open. Two live research pilots ran on one topic, and both failed the criterion that a stop candidate forms before the iteration cap: each run stopped on `maxIterationsReached`. The criterion was then amended to a frontier drain before the cap, which the second run met: its frontier showed 0 open nodes from the check before iteration 15 of 16. This page describes the shipped code and its tests.

---

## 2. HOW IT WORKS

### Seeding And Admission

The expansive run seeds depth-one nodes before iteration 1. Each iteration's leaf `frontier_proposal` rows go through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs`, which admits each proposal into the node store. It applies the relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle checks in that order, and records the name of the first failing check as the reason for each prune.

### Configuration

`expansiveDepth` in `deep-research-config.json` sets `maxDepth` to 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` to 6, a 50/35/15 iteration budget across depths, and `maxFrontierNodes` to null, which admission resolves to half of `maxIterations` (10 when no config exists yet, as on a fan-out parent). The depth-1 seed answers only to the root-children cap, but every seed that is not pruned counts toward the run cap. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(10, 2 × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes, 16 at the full six seeds, and the seed step prints the derived budget. The node cap stays half that budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. `antiConvergence.depthMode` defaults to `default`. The convergence script adds `depthMode` to its stop-decision config only when the value is `expansive`.

### Stop And Next Focus

While a frontier node is open, a stop candidate, whether `all_questions_answered` or a composite convergence stop, becomes another iteration. Next Focus steers to the shallowest open node. The iteration cap, the anti-convergence floor, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off` and the max-iterations policy decide before the frontier veto, so the frontier never extends a run past them.

### Fan-Out

Fan-out lineages each receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to a lineage only when it is `expansive`. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers and runs a fan-in guard over the recorded partition.

// A leaf can reword a focus before it reaches the state log, so the marker alone
// cannot attribute every visit. A store row that marked the node active on an
// iteration's number claims that iteration's record either way.
function reviewRecordsForNode(nodeId, history, iterationRecords) {
  const marker = frontierMarkerPattern(nodeId);
  const activeIterations = new Set(
    (Array.isArray(history) ? history : [])
      .filter((row) => row && row.nodeId === nodeId && row.status === 'active')
      .map((row) => getIterationRun(row))
      .filter((iteration) => iteration !== null),
  );
  return (Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords : []).filter((record) => {
    if (!record || record.type !== 'iteration') return false;
    if (record.frontierNodeId === nodeId) return true;
    if (typeof record.focus === 'string' && marker.test(record.focus)) return true;
    const iteration = getIterationRun(record);
    return iteration !== null && activeIterations.has(iteration);
  });
}

// Only an attempt that produced evidence counts as a visit: an errored or timed
// out record is retried work, not depth the node has already spent.
function reviewFrontierVisits(nodeId, history, iterationRecords) {
  return reviewRecordsForNode(nodeId, history, iterationRecords)
    .filter((record) => !FRONTIER_FAILED_STATUSES.has(String(record.status || '').toLowerCase()))
    .length;
}

// Depth bands share maxIterations 50/35/15 by default (depths 0-1, 2, 3). A band's
// unspent share rolls down to the next band only once the band has no open node,
// and never up. When a band's spend reaches its share, its open nodes close as
// exhausted with reason budget, so the run stops waiting on work it cannot fund.
function applyReviewFrontierBudget(nodes, history, iterationRecords, maxIterations, budgetSplit) {
  const split = budgetSplit && typeof budgetSplit === 'object' ? budgetSplit : {};
  const shares = [
    Number.isFinite(split.depth1) ? split.depth1 : 0.5,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth2) ? split.depth2 : 0.35,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth3) ? split.depth3 : 0.15,
  ];
  const cap = Number.isFinite(maxIterations) && maxIterations > 0 ? maxIterations : 10;
  const bandOf = (depth) => (depth <= 1 ? 0 : depth === 2 ? 1 : 2);
  const spent = [0, 0, 0];
  for (const node of nodes) {
    spent[bandOf(node.depth)] += reviewFrontierVisits(node.nodeId, history, iterationRecords);
  }
  let result = nodes.map((node) => ({ ...node }));
  const budget = {};
  let carry = 0;
  for (let band = 0; band < 3; band += 1) {
    const allotted = cap * shares[band] + carry;
    if (spent[band] >= allotted) {
      result = result.map((node) => (bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active')
        ? { ...node, status: 'exhausted', reason: 'budget' }
        : node));
    }
    const bandOpen = result.some((node) => bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active'));
    carry = bandOpen ? 0 : Math.max(0, allotted - spent[band]);
    budget[`depth${band + 1}`] = { allotted: Math.round(allotted * 100) / 100, spent: spent[band] };
  }
  return { nodes: result, budget };
}

// A node is answered once every dimension in the review's universe has evidence
// behind it. That status is derived on every reduce instead of written back, so
// the store keeps only the lifecycle its writer owns and the projection stays a
// pure function of the rows it read.
function buildReviewFrontier(storeData, iterationRecords, config) {
  const universe = buildDimensionUniverse(config);
  const history = Array.isArray(storeData?.history) ? storeData.history : [];
  const storedNodes = Array.isArray(storeData?.nodes) ? storeData.nodes : [];
  const nodes = storedNodes.map((node) => {
    const nodeId = node.nodeId;
    const records = reviewRecordsForNode(nodeId, history, iterationRecords)
      .filter((record) => !FRONTIER_FAILED_STATUSES.has(String(record.status || '').toLowerCase()));
    const covered = new Set();
    for (const record of records) {
      if (!Array.isArray(record.dimensions)) continue;
      for (const dimension of record.dimensions) {
        const name = String(dimension).trim().toLowerCase();
        if (name) covered.add(name);
      }
    }
    const coveredDimensions = universe.filter((dimension) => covered.has(dimension));
    const stillOpen = node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active';
    const status = stillOpen && coveredDimensions.length === universe.length ? 'answered' : node.status;
    return {
      nodeId,
      parentId: node.parentId ?? null,
      depth: node.depth,
      scope: node.scope ?? null,
      question: node.question ?? null,
      status,
      reason: status === node.status ? (node.reason ?? null) : null,
      iteration: node.iteration ?? null,
      coveredDimensions,
      nextDimension: universe.find((dimension) => !covered.has(dimension)) ?? null,
      visits: records.length,
    };
  });
  const budgeted = applyReviewFrontierBudget(
    nodes,
    history,
    iterationRecords,
    Number(config?.maxIterations),
    config?.expansiveDepth?.budgetSplit,
  );
  const projected = budgeted.nodes;
  const stats = { total: projected.length };
  for (const status of FRONTIER_STATUSES) stats[status] = projected.filter((node) => node.status === status).length;
  // Breadth first: the shallowest node, then one not yet visited, then the oldest.
  const chosen = projected
    .filter((node) => node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active')
    .sort((left, right) => ((left.depth ?? 0) - (right.depth ?? 0))
      || (left.visits - right.visits)
      || ((left.iteration ?? 0) - (right.iteration ?? 0))
      || left.nodeId.localeCompare(right.nodeId))[0] ?? null;
  return {
    nodes: projected,
    stats,
    budget: budgeted.budget,
    activeNodeId: chosen ? chosen.nodeId : null,
    activeNextDimension: chosen ? chosen.nextDimension : null,
  };
}

function frontierScopeRefs(scope) {
  if (Array.isArray(scope)) return asStringList(scope);
  const single = normalizeText(scope);
  return single ? [single] : [];
}

// A node the store still holds open is search depth the run has not spent yet,
// so it belongs in the same debt ledger a leaf's own deferral lands in. Exhausted
// and blocked nodes stay frontier-only: their question is closed, and filing them
// as debt would report work the run is no longer allowed to do.
function mergeFrontierIntoSearchLedger(searchLedgerState, frontier) {
  const candidateCoverage = searchLedgerState.candidateCoverage;
  const debtById = new Map(
    (Array.isArray(searchLedgerState.searchDebt) ? searchLedgerState.searchDebt : [])
      .map((entry) => [entry.id, entry]),
  );
  for (const node of frontier.nodes) {
    const bugClass = `frontier:${node.nodeId}`;
    const iteration = node.iteration ?? 0;
    if (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active') {
      const id = `frontier-${node.nodeId}`;
      debtById.set(id, {
        id,
        iteration,
        targetRefs: frontierScopeRefs(node.scope),
[0m$ [0msed -n '2480,2640p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs
.filter((record) => !FRONTIER_FAILED_STATUSES.has(String(record?.status || '').toLowerCase()))
    .filter((record) => typeof record?.focus === 'string' && marker.test(record.focus))
    .length;
}

function pickFrontierFocus(frontier, iterationRecords) {
  const open = (Array.isArray(frontier?.nodes) ? frontier.nodes : [])
    .filter((node) => node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active');
  if (open.length === 0) return '';
  // Breadth first: the shallowest node, then one not yet visited, then the oldest.
  const ranked = open
    .map((node) => ({ node, visits: frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, iterationRecords) }))
    .sort((left, right) => (left.node.depth - right.node.depth)
      || (left.visits - right.visits)
      || ((left.node.iteration ?? 0) - (right.node.iteration ?? 0))
      || left.node.nodeId.localeCompare(right.node.nodeId));
  const chosen = ranked[0].node;
  const scope = Array.isArray(chosen.scope) ? chosen.scope.join(', ') : (chosen.scope || 'unscoped');
  return `Frontier node ${chosen.nodeId} (depth ${chosen.depth}, scope ${scope}): ${chosen.question}`;
}

// Depth bands share maxIterations 50/35/15 by default (depths 0-1, 2, 3). A band's
// unspent share rolls down to the next band only once the band has no open node,
// and never up. When a band's spend reaches its share, its open nodes close as
// exhausted with reason budget, so the run stops waiting on work it cannot fund.
// An open node whose question an iteration already answered is marked answered
// first: the budget closes only work still outstanding, and an answered node
// never holds its band open against the roll-down.
function applyFrontierBudget(nodes, iterationRecords, maxIterations, budgetSplit, answeredNodeIds = new Set()) {
  const split = budgetSplit && typeof budgetSplit === 'object' ? budgetSplit : {};
  const shares = [
    Number.isFinite(split.depth1) ? split.depth1 : 0.5,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth2) ? split.depth2 : 0.35,
    Number.isFinite(split.depth3) ? split.depth3 : 0.15,
  ];
  const cap = Number.isFinite(maxIterations) && maxIterations > 0 ? maxIterations : 10;
  const bandOf = (depth) => (depth <= 1 ? 0 : depth === 2 ? 1 : 2);
  const spent = [0, 0, 0];
  for (const node of nodes) {
    const visits = frontierNodeVisits(node.nodeId, iterationRecords);
    spent[bandOf(node.depth)] += visits;
  }
  let result = nodes.map((node) => ((node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active') && answeredNodeIds.has(node.nodeId)
    ? { ...node, status: 'answered', reason: null }
    : { ...node }));
  const budget = {};
  let carry = 0;
  for (let band = 0; band < 3; band += 1) {
    const allotted = cap * shares[band] + carry;
    if (spent[band] >= allotted) {
      result = result.map((node) => (bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active')
        ? { ...node, status: 'exhausted', reason: 'budget' }
        : node));
    }
    const bandOpen = result.some((node) => bandOf(node.depth) === band && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active'));
    carry = bandOpen ? 0 : Math.max(0, allotted - spent[band]);
    budget[`depth${band + 1}`] = { allotted: Math.round(allotted * 100) / 100, spent: spent[band] };
  }
  return { nodes: result, budget };
}

function resolveNextFocus(registry, iterationFiles, iterationRecords) {
  const rejectedIndex = registry.rejectedPatternIndex;
  const latestBlockedStop = Array.isArray(registry.blockedStopHistory)
    ? registry.blockedStopHistory.at(-1)
    : null;
  const latestIteration = Array.isArray(iterationRecords) ? iterationRecords.at(-1) : null;

  if (registry.divergence?.currentFocus
    && isFiniteNumber(registry.divergence.activeSourceIteration)
    && registry.divergence.activeSourceIteration > (readIterationNumber(latestIteration) ?? 0)) {
    return registry.divergence.currentFocus;
  }

  if (latestBlockedStop) {
    const latestBlockedTimestamp = getTimestampValue(latestBlockedStop.timestamp);
    const latestIterationTimestamp = getTimestampValue(latestIteration?.timestamp);

    if (latestBlockedTimestamp > latestIterationTimestamp) {
      const recoveryStrategy = latestBlockedStop.recoveryStrategy || '[No recovery strategy recorded]';
      const recoveryMatch = findRejectedPatternMatch(
        { text: recoveryStrategy, category: 'recovery' },
        rejectedIndex,
        { category: 'recovery' },
      );
      if (!recoveryMatch) {
        return [
          `BLOCKED on: ${formatBlockedByList(latestBlockedStop.blockedBy)}`,
          `Recovery: ${recoveryStrategy}`,
          'Address the blocking gates before the next iteration.',
        ].join('\n');
      }
    }
  }

  // Under expansive depth mode an open frontier node outranks the continuity
  // focus; the pivot focus and blocked-stop recovery above still come first.
  if (registry.frontier) {
    const frontierFocus = pickFrontierFocus(registry.frontier, iterationRecords);
    if (frontierFocus) return frontierFocus;
  }

  const filteredInputs = filterNextFocusInputs(registry, iterationFiles, iterationRecords);
  // A leaf recommendation goes through the same rejected-pattern suppression as every other next-focus candidate.
  const latestLeafNextFocus = [...(Array.isArray(filteredInputs.iterationFiles) ? filteredInputs.iterationFiles : [])]
    .sort((left, right) => (left.run || 0) - (right.run || 0))
    .map((iteration) => iteration.nextFocus)
    .filter((value) => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim())
    .filter((value) => !findRejectedPatternMatch({ text: value, category: 'next-focus' }, rejectedIndex, { category: 'next-focus' }))
    .at(-1) || '';
  const continuityFocus = deriveNextFocusFromContinuity({
    iterationFiles: filteredInputs.iterationFiles,
    iterationRecords: filteredInputs.iterationRecords,
    carriedForwardOpenQuestions: filteredInputs.carriedForwardOpenQuestions,
    machineOpenQuestions: filteredInputs.openQuestions,
    leafNextFocus: latestLeafNextFocus,
    resolvedQuestions: Array.isArray(registry.resolvedQuestions) ? registry.resolvedQuestions : [],
  });

  const normalizedFocus = normalizeOptionalText(continuityFocus).toLowerCase();
  const shouldUseIdeaFocus = !normalizedFocus
    || normalizedFocus === '[none yet]'
    || normalizedFocus === '[all tracked questions are resolved]';
  if (!shouldUseIdeaFocus) {
    return continuityFocus;
  }

  const promotedIdea = Array.isArray(registry.promotedIdeas)
    ? registry.promotedIdeas[0]
    : null;
  return promotedIdea?.idea || continuityFocus;
}

function buildRegistry(strategyQuestions, iterationFiles, iterationRecords, eventRecords, reducerState = {}) {
  const questionCoverageRecords = iterationRecords.filter((record) => {
    const status = normalizeText(String(record.status || 'complete')).toLowerCase();
    return !['error', 'failed', 'failure', 'stuck', 'thought'].includes(status);
  });

  const resolutionRecords = strategyQuestions.map((question) => {
    const normalized = normalizeText(question.text);
    return questionCoverageRecords.find((record) => {
      const answeredQuestions = Array.isArray(record.answeredQuestions)
        ? record.answeredQuestions.map(normalizeText)
        : [];
      const keyQuestions = Array.isArray(record.keyQuestions)
        ? record.keyQuestions.map(normalizeText)
        : [];
      return answeredQuestions.includes(normalized)
        || (answeredQuestions.length > 0 && keyQuestions.length === 1 && keyQuestions[0] === normalized)
        || normalizeOptionalText(record.focus) === normalized;
    });
  });

  // The budget runs after the iteration evidence, so a node whose question is
  // already answered is never closed as budget-exhausted.
  const frontierState = reducerState.frontier

function blockerName(blocker) {
  if (typeof blocker === 'string') return blocker;
  if (blocker && typeof blocker === 'object' && typeof blocker.type === 'string') {
    return blocker.type;
  }
  return 'graph_blocker';
}

function combineGraphDecision(candidate, graphDecision, graphBlockers) {
  const reasonDetail = candidate && typeof candidate.reasonDetail === 'string'
    ? candidate.reasonDetail
    : 'composite_converged';

  if (graphDecision === 'STOP_ALLOWED') {
    return { decision: 'STOP', stopReason: 'converged', reasonDetail, blockedBy: [] };
  }
  if (graphDecision === 'STOP_BLOCKED') {
    const blockers = Array.isArray(graphBlockers) ? graphBlockers.map(blockerName) : [];
    return {
      decision: 'BLOCKED',
      stopReason: 'blockedStop',
      reasonDetail: 'graphStopBlocked',
      blockedBy: blockers.length > 0 ? blockers : ['graph_blocker'],
    };
  }
  if (graphDecision === 'CONTINUE') {
    return {
      decision: 'CONTINUE',
      stopReason: null,
      reasonDetail: 'graph_convergence_continue',
      blockedBy: [],
    };
  }
  return {
    decision: 'CONTINUE',
    stopReason: null,
    reasonDetail: 'graphDecisionMissing',
    blockedBy: [],
  };
}

function buildStopDecision(input) {
  const source = input && typeof input === 'object' ? input : {};
  const loopType = source.loopType;
  const config = source.config && typeof source.config === 'object' ? source.config : {};
  const research = source.research && typeof source.research === 'object' ? source.research : {};
  const review = source.review && typeof source.review === 'object' ? source.review : {};
  const ratios = Array.isArray(source.ratios) ? source.ratios : [];
  const iterationCount = typeof source.iterationCount === 'number' ? source.iterationCount : 0;
  const stuckCount = typeof source.stuckCount === 'number' ? source.stuckCount : 0;
  const consecutiveFailureCount = typeof source.consecutiveFailureCount === 'number'
    ? source.consecutiveFailureCount
    : 0;
  const maxIterations = Number.isFinite(config.maxIterations) ? config.maxIterations : Infinity;
  const stuckThreshold = Number.isFinite(config.stuckThreshold) ? config.stuckThreshold : Infinity;
  const minIterations = Number.isFinite(config.minIterations) ? config.minIterations : 0;
  const missingDimensions = Array.isArray(review.missingDimensions) ? review.missingDimensions : [];
  const totalDimensions = typeof review.totalDimensions === 'number' ? review.totalDimensions : 0;
  const coverage = loopType === 'review'
    ? {
      covered: totalDimensions - missingDimensions.length,
      total: totalDimensions,
      traceabilityCovered: review.traceabilityCovered,
      coverageAge: review.coverageAge,
    }
    : {
      answered: typeof research.answeredCount === 'number' ? research.answeredCount : 0,
      total: typeof research.totalQuestions === 'number' ? research.totalQuestions : 0,
    };

  const signalResult = computeStopSignals({
    loopType,
    ratios,
    convergenceThreshold: config.convergenceThreshold,
    coverage,
  });

  const qualityGuards = loopType === 'research'
    ? evaluateResearchQualityGuards({
      answeredQuestionSourceCounts: research.answeredQuestionSourceCounts,
      iterationRecords: research.iterationRecords,
    })
    : null;
  const failedGuards = qualityGuards
    ? Object.keys(qualityGuards.guards).filter((name) => !qualityGuards.guards[name].passed)
    : [];

  const gatesAt = (hardStop) => {
    if (loopType === 'review') {
      const result = evaluateReviewLegalStopGates(review, {
        weightedStopScore: signalResult.weightedStopScore,
        hardStop,
      });
      return { gates: result.gates, failed: result.failed };
    }
    return { gates: { qualityGuards }, failed: failedGuards };
  };

  const emit = (decision, stopReason, reasonDetail, evaluated, blockedBy) => ({
    decision,
    stopReason,
    reasonDetail,
    weightedStopScore: signalResult.weightedStopScore,
    signals: signalResult.signals,
    gates: evaluated.gates,
    blockedBy,
    graphDecision: source.graphDecision ?? null,
    inputs: input,
  });

  const cleanReviewStop = loopType === 'review'
    && missingDimensions.length === 0
    && review.traceabilityCovered === true
    && review.activeP0 === 0
    && review.activeP1 === 0
    && review.coverageAge >= 1;

  // The cap is terminal and outranks every veto; failed guards or gates are
  // reported as evidence instead of changing the outcome.
  if (iterationCount >= maxIterations) {
    const capGates = gatesAt(true);
    return emit(
      'STOP',
      'maxIterationsReached',
      'maxIterationsReached',
      capGates,
      capGates.failed,
    );
  }

  if (consecutiveFailureCount >= CONSECUTIVE_FAILURE_LIMIT) {
    return emit('STOP', 'error', 'consecutiveFailures', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  if (stuckCount >= stuckThreshold) {
    return emit('STUCK_RECOVERY', 'stuckRecovery', 'stuckThresholdReached', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  if (config.convergenceMode === 'off') {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'convergenceModeOff', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  if (config.stopPolicy === 'max-iterations') {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'stopPolicyMaxIterations', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  let candidate = null;
  if (loopType === 'review') {
    if (cleanReviewStop) {
      candidate = { reasonDetail: 'all_dimensions_clean', hardStop: true };
    } else if (signalResult.stopCandidate) {
      candidate = { reasonDetail: 'composite_converged', hardStop: false };
    } else if (config.depthMode === 'expansive'
      && Number.isFinite(review.frontierTotal)
      && review.frontierTotal >= 1
      && review.frontierOpen === 0
      && Number.isFinite(review.frontierAnswered)
      && review.frontierAnswered >= 1) {
      // An expansive review whose frontier holds nodes, none still open or
      // active and at least one answered has no frontier work left; a fan-out
      // lineage reads only its own partition. Without this candidate the run
      // goes to the cap even after its nodes are answered. A frontier that
      // closed with nothing answered (every node exhausted or pruned) forms no
      // candidate. It is a hard stop because a soft one passes the convergence
      // gate only where the composite vote already forms a candidate; every
      // other legal-stop gate still applies.
      candidate = { reasonDetail: 'frontier_drained', hardStop: true };
    }
  } else if (coverage.total > 0 && coverage.answered >= coverage.total) {
    candidate = { reasonDetail: 'all_questions_answered', hardStop: false };
  } else if (signalResult.stopCandidate) {
    candidate = { reasonDetail: 'composite_converged', hardStop: false };
  }

  if (!candidate) {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'noStopCandidate', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  if (minIterations > 1 && iterationCount < minIterations) {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'minIterationsNotReached', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  // An expansive run that still holds open frontier nodes turns a stop
  // candidate into another iteration. It never creates a stop, and everything
  // above it (cap, failures, stuck recovery, off, max-iterations, the floor)
  // still wins. A review clean candidate is a hard stop, so keying the veto on
  // soft candidates alone would leave a review run no way to spend the
  // iteration its open nodes need.
  const depthFacts = loopType === 'review' ? review : research;
  const vetoable = loopType === 'review' || !candidate.hardStop;
  if (config.depthMode === 'expansive'
    && vetoable
    && Number.isFinite(depthFacts.frontierOpen)
    && depthFacts.frontierOpen > 0) {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'frontierOpen', gatesAt(false), []);
  }

  const evaluated = gatesAt(candidate.hardStop);
  if (loopType === 'review' && evaluated.failed.length > 0) {
    return emit('BLOCKED', 'blockedStop', evaluated.failed[0], evaluated, evaluated.failed);
  }
  if (loopType !== 'review' && failedGuards.length > 0) {
    return emit('CONTINUE', null, 'qualityGuardFailed', evaluated, failedGuards);
  }

  const combined = combineGraphDecision(candidate, source.graphDecision, source.graphBlockers);
  return emit(
    combined.decision,
    combined.stopReason,
    combined.reasonDetail,
    evaluated,
    combined.blockedBy,
  );
}

// The stop decision reads loop state from files, so these helpers stay
// independent of the coverage graph and remain usable when the graph fails.
function isPlainObject(value) {
  return Boolean(value) && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function finiteNumberOrNull(value) {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null;
}

function stopDecisionNumberFlag(value, key) {
  if (value === undefined || value === null || value === true || value === '') return null;
  const parsed = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(parsed)) throw inputError(`${key} must be a finite number`);
  return Math.trunc(parsed);
}

function readJsonObjectFile(filePath, key) {
  if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) throw inputError(`${key} file does not exist: ${filePath}`);
  let raw;
  try {
    raw = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
  } catch {
    throw inputError(`${key} file is not readable: ${filePath}`);
  }
  let parsed;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch {
    throw inputError(`${key} file must be JSON: ${filePath}`);
  }
  if (!isPlainObject(parsed)) throw inputError(`${key} file must hold a JSON object: ${filePath}`);
  return parsed;
}

  for (const line of raw.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    if (line.trim() === '') continue;
    lineCount += 1;
    try {

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md:127:`--depth-mode=expansive` lets a run grow its own questions. A seed step writes 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1, and the leaf may propose children only when its prompt carries a frontier block. An admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, and prunes the proposal on the first failure. Next Focus reads the shallowest open node first and writes it as a frontier line, so visiting a node does not read as answering it. While a node stays open the frontier vetoes a soft convergence stop, which never outranks the iteration cap or the other hard stops, and the caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations`. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(10, 2 × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes, 16 at the full six seeds, and the seed step prints the derived budget. The node cap stays half that budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md:158:`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. With the four canonical dimensions the ceiling of 24 binds from two seeds on, so one run cannot fund every node at every depth of a wide review. Split a wide review with fan-out, where each lineage sizes its own budget from its share of the seed. A lineage dealt no seed node, or a run whose seeds were all pruned, runs at its loop default instead: 12 iterations for a native lineage and 7 for a CLI one or a single run. The node cap stays half the budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. At about seven to eight minutes an iteration, a 24-iteration review can reach `maxDurationMinutes` (120) before its iteration cap.

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:459:Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations` unless the config sets a number. The depth-1 seed answers only to the root-children cap, but every seed that is not pruned counts toward the run cap. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(7, 2 × D × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes and D configured dimensions, and the seed step prints the derived budget. With the four canonical dimensions the ceiling of 24 binds from two seeds on, so one run cannot fund every node at every depth of a wide review; split a wide review with fan-out, where each lineage sizes its own budget from its share of the seed. A lineage dealt no seed node, or a run whose seeds were all pruned, runs at its loop default instead: 12 iterations for a native lineage and 7 for a CLI one or a single run. The node cap stays half the budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. At about seven to eight minutes an iteration, a 24-iteration review can reach `maxDurationMinutes` (120) before its iteration cap. A run without the flag keeps the default path and runs exactly as before, and the mode is locked against the optimizer; the `expansiveDepth` caps are in neither its tunable nor its locked list.

// A review node costs one iteration per configured dimension, so its derived
// budget scales with the dimension count and reaches the ceiling fast.
describe('review expansive budget scales with the dimension count', () => {
  type SeedBudget = AdmissionResult & { derivedMaxIterations: number; growthSlots: number };
  const seedWithBudget = seedNodes as unknown as (options: {
    artifactDir: string;
    iteration?: number;
    candidates: unknown[];
    target: string;
    maxIterationsSource?: string;
  }) => SeedBudget;

  function reviewDir(reviewDimensions: string[]): string {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'frontier-admit-review-budget-'));
    tempDirs.push(dir);
    writeFileSync(join(dir, REVIEW_CONFIG_FILE), JSON.stringify({ maxIterations: 7, reviewDimensions, expansiveDepth: { maxDepth: 3 } }), 'utf8');
    return dir;
  }

  const parts = (count: number) => Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    question: `what does review part ${index + 1} hide`,
    scope: `src/part-${index + 1}`,
    evidence: `src/part-${index + 1}/index.ts:1`,
  }));

  it('one dimension sizes the budget like research', () => {
    const result = seedWithBudget({ artifactDir: reviewDir(['correctness']), candidates: parts(3), target: 'review', maxIterationsSource: 'default' });
    expect(result.derivedMaxIterations).toBe(10);
    expect(result.growthSlots).toBe(2);
  });

  it('four dimensions hit the ceiling of 24 from two seeds on', () => {
    const one = seedWithBudget({ artifactDir: reviewDir(['correctness', 'security', 'traceability', 'maintainability']), candidates: parts(1), target: 'review', maxIterationsSource: 'default' });
    expect(one.derivedMaxIterations).toBe(24);
    const two = seedWithBudget({ artifactDir: reviewDir(['correctness', 'security', 'traceability', 'maintainability']), candidates: parts(2), target: 'review', maxIterationsSource: 'default' });
    expect(two.derivedMaxIterations).toBe(24);
    expect(two.growthSlots).toBe(10);
  });
});

function normalizeDepthMode(raw) {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === false || raw === null || raw === '' || raw === 'default') return null;
  if (raw !== 'expansive') throw inputError('depthMode must be one of: default, expansive');
  return raw;
}

// Open depth-1 nodes are dealt round-robin in node-id order, so the same seed
// always gives each lineage the same share and the merge guard can check it.
function partitionFrontierNodes(baseArtifactDir, labels) {
  const { readNodeStore } = require('./frontier-admit.cjs');
  const nodes = [...readNodeStore(path.join(baseArtifactDir, 'frontier-nodes.jsonl')).values()]
    .filter((node) => node.depth === 1 && (node.status === 'open' || node.status === 'active'))
    .sort((left, right) => left.nodeId.localeCompare(right.nodeId));
  const partition = Object.fromEntries(labels.map((label) => [label, []]));
  nodes.forEach((node, index) => { partition[labels[index % labels.length]].push(node); });
  return partition;
}

// Under expansive depth a lineage the matrix gave no iterations is sized from its own
// share of the seed, the way a single-executor run is sized after its seed step. The
// floor is the budget that lineage would otherwise have run: 12 for a native lineage,
// the workflow default for a CLI one, and a lineage dealt no seed node runs at that
// floor. Every such lineage gets an explicit iteration count, so a max-iterations
// stop policy never finds it uncapped. A review lineage reviews the canonical four
// dimensions. A matrix iterations value is explicit and always kept.
function expansiveLineageBudgets(lineages, { loopType, partition }) {
  const { expansiveBudget, LOOP_DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS } = require('./frontier-admit.cjs');
  return lineages.map((lineage) => {
    if (Number.isInteger(lineage.iterations) && lineage.iterations > 0) return lineage;
    const share = partition && Array.isArray(partition[lineage.label]) ? partition[lineage.label].length : 0;
    const iterations = expansiveBudget({
      target: loopType,
      seeded: share,
      loopDefault: lineage.kind === 'native' ? 12 : LOOP_DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS[loopType],
    });
    return { ...lineage, iterations };
  });
}

// The stderr lines that explain each derived lineage budget. A lineage dealt no seed
// node is named as such, and one warning says when there are more lineages than open
// seed nodes, since the extra lineages have no frontier to work.
function expansiveLineageBudgetNotes(expanded, budgeted, partition) {
  const shareOf = (label) => (partition && Array.isArray(partition[label]) ? partition[label].length : 0);
  const seedCount = expanded.reduce((total, lineage) => total + shareOf(lineage.label), 0);
  const notes = [];
  if (expanded.length > seedCount) {
    const idle = expanded.length - seedCount;
    notes.push(`fanout-run: ${expanded.length} lineages share ${seedCount} open seed node${seedCount === 1 ? '' : 's'}, `
      + `so ${idle} lineage${idle === 1 ? '' : 's'} get${idle === 1 ? 's' : ''} no seed share`);
  }
  budgeted.forEach((lineage, index) => {
    if (lineage === expanded[index]) return;
    notes.push(shareOf(lineage.label) === 0
      ? `fanout-run: lineage ${lineage.label} got no seed share and runs at its loop default of ${lineage.iterations} iterations`
      : `fanout-run: lineage ${lineage.label} budget ${lineage.iterations} iterations, derived from its seed share`);
  });
  return notes;
}

// A lineage starts from its own store and inbox. They are written only when the
// lineage has no store yet, so a resumed lineage keeps the nodes it already closed.
// Review carries no inbox: its nodes reach the leaf through the FRONTIER block, so
// a seeded inbox would only hand the finding pipeline a file nothing reads.
function writeLineageFrontierStore(lineageDir, nodes, options = {}) {
  const storePath = path.join(lineageDir, 'frontier-nodes.jsonl');
  if (fs.existsSync(storePath) || nodes.length === 0) return false;
  fs.mkdirSync(lineageDir, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(storePath, nodes.map((node) => `${JSON.stringify(node)}\n`).join(''), 'utf8');
  if (options.inbox === false) return true;
  const inboxRows = nodes.map((node) => JSON.stringify({ id: node.nodeId, text: node.question, source: 'frontier', origin: 'frontier', injectedAtIteration: 0 }));
  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(lineageDir, 'inbox.jsonl'), `${inboxRows.join('\n')}\n`, 'utf8');
  return true;
}

function normalizeStopPolicy(raw) {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === false || raw === null || raw === '') {
    return 'convergence';
  }
  if (raw === true || typeof raw !== 'string') {
    throw inputError('stopPolicy must be convergence or max-iterations');
  }
  if (raw !== 'convergence' && raw !== 'max-iterations') {
    throw inputError('stopPolicy must be convergence or max-iterations');
  }
  return raw;
}

// Returns null when the flag is absent, so the fan-out config value can supply the mode
// instead. Preserve is the default because the guard cannot prove which writer made an
// out-of-scope change on a shared checkout, and a restore acts on that guess by overwriting
// the bytes on disk; restore stays reachable only by asking for it, here or in the config.
function normalizeContainmentMode(raw) {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === false || raw === null || raw === '') {
    return null;
  }
  if (raw === true || typeof raw !== 'string') {
    throw inputError('containmentMode must be preserve or restore');
  }
  if (raw !== 'preserve' && raw !== 'restore') {
    throw inputError('containmentMode must be preserve or restore');
  }
  return raw;
}

[0m$ [0msed -n '740,820p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs; sed -n '1730,1790p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs
function buildLineageExecutors(lineageData) {
  const executors = {};
  const sorted = [...lineageData].sort((a, b) => (a.label < b.label ? -1 : a.label > b.label ? 1 : 0));
  for (const { label, kind, model, reasoningEffort } of sorted) {
    executors[label] = {
      kind: kind ?? 'unknown',
      model: model ?? 'unknown',
      reasoningEffort: reasoningEffort ?? null,
    };
  }
  return executors;
}

/**
 * Merge research findings registries from all lineages.
 * Deduplicates by findingId; cross-model attribution via lineage labels.
 * Returns the merged registry object.
 */
const FRONTIER_STATUSES = ['open', 'active', 'answered', 'blocked', 'exhausted', 'abstained', 'pruned'];

// Lineages number the nodes they admit from their own store, so two lineages
// that each hold one dealt node both name their first child F-2-002. A merged
// node id is therefore qualified by its lineage label, its parent link with it,
// and the lineage's own id stays beside it. The partition guard reads the depth-1
// split the fan-out runner recorded against those lineage ids: a node dealt to a
// lineage that no lineage reports was lost, and the guard names it instead of
// letting the merged frontier look complete.
function qualifyFrontierId(label, nodeId) {
  return typeof nodeId === 'string' && nodeId !== '' && nodeId !== 'root' ? `${label}:${nodeId}` : nodeId;
}

function mergeResearchFrontier(lineageData, frontierPartition) {
  const nodes = [];
  for (const { label, registry } of [...lineageData].sort((left, right) => compareText(left.label, right.label))) {
    const lineageNodes = Array.isArray(registry?.frontier?.nodes) ? registry.frontier.nodes : [];
    for (const node of lineageNodes) {
      nodes.push({
        ...node,
        nodeId: qualifyFrontierId(label, node.nodeId),
        lineageNodeId: node.nodeId,
        ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(node, 'parentId') ? { parentId: qualifyFrontierId(label, node.parentId) } : {}),
        lineage: label,
      });
    }
  }
  nodes.sort((left, right) => compareText(left.lineage, right.lineage) || compareText(left.lineageNodeId, right.lineageNodeId));
  const stats = { total: nodes.length };
  for (const status of FRONTIER_STATUSES) stats[status] = nodes.filter((node) => node.status === status).length;
  const reported = new Set(nodes.map((node) => node.lineageNodeId));
  const expected = frontierPartition && typeof frontierPartition === 'object'
    ? Object.values(frontierPartition).flatMap((ids) => (Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : []))
    : [];
  const missing = [...new Set(expected.filter((id) => !reported.has(id)))].sort(compareText);
  return {
    nodes,
    stats,
    ...(expected.length > 0 ? { partitionGuard: { passed: missing.length === 0, missing } } : {}),
  };
}

function mergeResearchRegistries(lineageData, options = {}) {
  const mergeOptions = resolveMergeOptions(options);
  const findingById = mergeOptions.enableNearDuplicateDedup ? createFindingBucketIndex() : new Map();
  const schemaWarnings = [];

  for (const { label, registry: rawRegistry } of lineageData) {
    const { registry, warnings } = normalizeRegistrySchema(rawRegistry, {
      canonicalKey: 'keyFindings',
      aliases: { findings: 'keyFindings' },
      lineage: label,
    });
    for (const w of warnings) {
      schemaWarnings.push(w);
      process.stderr.write(JSON.stringify(w) + '\n');
    }
    if (!registry || !Array.isArray(registry.keyFindings)) continue;
    for (const finding of registry.keyFindings) {
      const id = finding.id || finding.title;
      if (!id) continue;
      if (mergeOptions.enableNearDuplicateDedup) {
        addResearchFinding(getFindingBucket(findingById, id, finding, true).records, finding, label, mergeOptions);

  const lineagesWithRegistry = lineageData.filter(
    (entry) => entry.registry !== null && !refusedProvenance.has(entry.label),
  );

  let mergedRegistry;
  if (loopType === 'review') {
    mergedRegistry = mergeReviewRegistries(lineagesWithRegistry, {
      ...resolveMergeOptions(args),
      frontierPartition: orchestrationSummary.frontier_partition ?? null,
    });
  } else {
    mergedRegistry = mergeResearchRegistries(lineagesWithRegistry, {
      ...resolveMergeOptions(args),
      frontierPartition: orchestrationSummary.frontier_partition ?? null,
    });
  }

  // Write merged registry to base artifact dir (replacing single-executor path).
  // Atomic temp+fsync+rename so a mid-write kill never hands synthesis a
  // truncated registry — readers see the prior file or the complete new one.
  const mergedRegistryPath = path.join(artifactDir, registryName);
  let compatibilityRegistryPath = null;
  if (compatibilityRegistryName) {
    const serializedRegistry = `${JSON.stringify(mergedRegistry, null, 2)}\n`;
    compatibilityRegistryPath = path.join(artifactDir, compatibilityRegistryName);
    assertSafeOutputPath(artifactRoot, mergedRegistryPath, 'merged registry');
    assertSafeOutputPath(artifactRoot, compatibilityRegistryPath, 'compatibility registry');
    writeTextAtomic(mergedRegistryPath, serializedRegistry);
    writeTextAtomic(compatibilityRegistryPath, serializedRegistry);
  } else {
    assertSafeOutputPath(artifactRoot, mergedRegistryPath, 'merged registry');
    writeStateAtomic(mergedRegistryPath, mergedRegistry);
  }

  // Write attribution markdown atomically (same torn-write guarantee; text, not JSON).
  const attributionPath = path.join(artifactDir, 'fanout-attribution.md');
  assertSafeOutputPath(artifactRoot, attributionPath, 'fan-out attribution');
  writeTextAtomic(attributionPath, buildAttributionMd(lineageData, loopType, { refusedLineages: refusedProvenance }));

  if (mergedRegistry.frontier?.partitionGuard && !mergedRegistry.frontier.partitionGuard.passed) {
    process.stderr.write(`${JSON.stringify({ warning: 'frontier_partition_guard_failed', missing: mergedRegistry.frontier.partitionGuard.missing })}\n`);
  }

  jsonOut({
    status: 'ok',
    loop_type: loopType,
    merged_lineages: lineagesWithRegistry.length,
    skipped_no_registry: lineageData.filter((entry) => entry.registry === null).length,
    ...(refusedProvenance.size > 0 ? { refused_reducer_provenance: [...refusedProvenance].sort() } : {}),
    ...(unverifiedProvenance.length > 0 ? { reducer_provenance_warnings: [...unverifiedProvenance].sort() } : {}),
    merged_registry_path: mergedRegistryPath,
    ...(compatibilityRegistryPath ? { compatibility_registry_path: compatibilityRegistryPath } : {}),
    attribution_path: attributionPath,
    ...(reconstructionWarnings.length > 0 ? { reconstruction_warnings: reconstructionWarnings } : {}),
    ...(loopType === 'review'
      ? { merged_verdict: mergedRegistry.mergedVerdict, active_p0: mergedRegistry.activeP0, active_p1: mergedRegistry.activeP1, active_p2: mergedRegistry.activeP2, search_debt: mergedRegistry.searchDebt.length, failed_required_gate_ids: mergedRegistry.failedRequiredGateIds, ...(mergedRegistry.frontier?.partitionGuard ? { frontier_partition_guard: mergedRegistry.frontier.partitionGuard } : {}) }
      : {
        key_findings: mergedRegistry.keyFindings?.length ?? 0,
        ...(mergedRegistry.frontier?.partitionGuard ? { frontier_partition_guard: mergedRegistry.frontier.partitionGuard } : {}),
      }),
[0m$ [0msed -n '3700,3860p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs
let rawConfig;
  try {
    rawConfig = JSON.parse(fanoutConfigJson);
  } catch {
    throw inputError('fanoutConfigJson is not valid JSON');
  }

  const lineagesDir = path.join(baseArtifactDir, 'lineages');
  const ledgerPath = path.join(baseArtifactDir, 'orchestration-status.log');
  const summaryPath = path.join(baseArtifactDir, 'orchestration-summary.json');
  // This process appends to its own ledgers throughout every lineage's dispatch —
  // a heartbeat on a timer, plus each start and settle. They sit above every
  // lineage dir, so a tree diff attributes the supervisor's own bookkeeping to
  // whichever leaf happened to be running, and once these files are committed the
  // revert restores them from HEAD and erases the record of the run in progress.
  // Named as whole paths so a neighbour sharing a prefix stays guarded.
  const orchestratorOwnedPaths = [
    ledgerPath,
    summaryPath,
    observabilityPathForLedger(ledgerPath),
  ];
  // Repo root for write containment, resolved once for the whole run: the working
  // directory, unless an operator pins DEEP_LOOP_REPO_ROOT or the artifact tree
  // resolves into a different checkout via symlink. Every lineage lives under the
  // same tree, so this is the same for all of them (see runtime-bootstrap).
  const containmentRepoRoot = require('./runtime-bootstrap.cjs')
    .resolveContainmentRepoRoot(process.env, process.cwd(), {
      artifactDir: lineagesDir,
      gitToplevel: (dir) => containmentInternals.resolveGitToplevel(dir),
    });
  const runId = `${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
  const parsedFanoutConfig = parseFanoutConfig(rawConfig);
  // CLI flag first, then the fan-out config value, then the schema default. Mutable
  // because a detected second writer latches this run into preserve for good.
  let containmentMode = containmentModeOverride ?? parsedFanoutConfig.containment.mode ?? 'preserve';
  const containmentChurnThreshold = parsedFanoutConfig.containment.churnThreshold;
  // The cumulative arm needs no flag of its own: it takes the same config route the per-window
  // threshold already takes, and a caller who wants one arm and not the other sets the other
  // to 0.
  const containmentChurnCumulativeThreshold = parsedFanoutConfig.containment.churnCumulativeThreshold;

  const rawGuardConfig = rawConfigWithCliBudgetOverrides(rawConfig, args);

  const lineageBudgetGuards = normalizeLineageBudgetGuards(rawGuardConfig);
  const guardedAssignment = applyFlatPoolAssignmentGuard(parsedFanoutConfig);
  const fanoutConfig = guardedAssignment.config;

  const expansiveBudgets = depthMode === 'expansive' && ACTIVE_FANOUT_LOOP_TYPES.has(loopType);
  const budgetPartition = expansiveBudgets
    ? partitionFrontierNodes(baseArtifactDir, expandedLineages.map((lineage) => lineage.label))
    : null;
  const allLineages = expansiveBudgets
    ? expansiveLineageBudgets(expandedLineages, { loopType, partition: budgetPartition })
    : expandedLineages;
  if (expansiveBudgets) {
    for (const note of expansiveLineageBudgetNotes(expandedLineages, allLineages, budgetPartition)) {
      process.stderr.write(`${note}\n`);
    }
  }
  // One lineage is not a fan-out. A caller who lands here with one lineage has left the
  // workflow that owns the packet init, synthesis and save phases, and a CLI lineage
  // would then run every iteration in one process.
  if (ACTIVE_FANOUT_LOOP_TYPES.has(loopType)
    && allLineages.length === 1
    && args.allowSingleLineage !== true
    && args.allowSingleLineage !== 'true'
    && args.allowSingleLineage !== '1') {
    throw inputError(
      `fan-out refused: this ${loopType} executor matrix expands to one lineage. `
        + `Run the single-executor /deep:${loopType} workflow instead; it runs the packet `
        + 'init, synthesis and save phases, which this runner does not.',
    );
  }
  if ((loopType === 'research' || loopType === 'review') && stopPolicy === 'max-iterations') {
    const uncappedLineages = allLineages.filter((lineage) => (
      !Number.isInteger(lineage.iterations) || lineage.iterations <= 0
    ));
    if (uncappedLineages.length > 0) {
      const labels = uncappedLineages.map((lineage) => lineage.label).join(', ');
      throw inputError(
        `stopPolicy=max-iterations requires a positive lineage iteration cap for lineage(s): ${labels}`,
      );
    }
  }
  try {
    preflightFanoutCapabilities(allLineages);
  } catch (error) {
    throw inputError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
  }

  // Aggregate budget is a SEPARATE ceiling from the per-lineage cap enforced inside the
  // worker below — both must pass, neither replaces the other. Checked upfront (before any
  // lineage spawns) because it is fully computable from static config, so failing fast here
  // avoids burning real subprocess time on a run that was already over budget at submission.
  const aggregateBudgetDecision = evaluateAggregateBudgetCap({
    lineages: allLineages,
    guards: rawGuardConfig,
    maxRetries: fanoutConfig.maxRetries,
  });
  if (!aggregateBudgetDecision.continue_allowed) {
    appendFanoutStatusLedger(ledgerPath, {
      event: 'aggregate_budget_cap_exceeded',
      at: new Date().toISOString(),
      run_id: runId,
      loop_type: loopType,
      spec_folder: specFolder,
      severity: 'error',
      stop_reasons: aggregateBudgetDecision.stop_reasons,
      upper_bound: aggregateBudgetDecision.upper_bound,
    });
    throw inputError(
      `fan-out aggregate budget exceeded: ${aggregateBudgetDecision.upper_bound.estimated_cost_units} > `
        + `${aggregateBudgetDecision.upper_bound.max_aggregate_cost_units} cost units across `
        + `${aggregateBudgetDecision.upper_bound.lineage_count} lineage(s)`,
    );
  }

  const postExitGraceMs = normalizePostExitGraceMs(rawGuardConfig, progressHeartbeatMs);
  const slotIntervalMs = progressHeartbeatMs;

    rejections: guardedAssignment.rejections,
    ledgerPath,
    runId,
    loopType,
    specFolder,
  });

  // LEAF review agent with a full-loop prompt, which is invalid; keep full-loop

    : {};

      checkpointPath,
      ledgerPath,
      runId,
      loopType,
      specFolder,
      waitMs: args.preDispatchWaitMs,
    });

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md:469:On a fan-out run the depth-1 nodes are dealt round-robin in node-id order, `frontier_scope:` binds each lineage's node ids beside `review_focus:` in its prompt, and the partition is recorded as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md:36:Fan-out lineages each receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-run.cjs` passes `depthMode` to a lineage only when it is `expansive`. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers and runs a fan-in guard over the recorded partition.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md:53:| `.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` | Script | Merges the lineage frontiers and runs the partition guard. |
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md:16:`--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A review without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` seeds depth-one sub-target nodes from the review target before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows under the same caps as research, keeps node status in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, and holds open nodes as `searchDebt`. Two live review pilots ran. In the first the depth-1 budget check passed but the stop criterion failed: the root fan-out cap of 6, since raised to 8 for a review seed, pruned a seventh depth-1 node, and three of six lineages stopped on `maxIterationsReached`. The second, at `maxIterations` 16, seeded eight depth-1 nodes and answered all eight, and the merged frontier ended with 0 open and 0 active nodes under a passing partition guard, which met the amended pilot criterion. This page describes the shipped code and its tests.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/feature-catalog/loop-lifecycle/expansive-depth-mode.md:40:Fan-out lineages receive a share of the seeded nodes. `runtime/scripts/fanout-merge.cjs` returns the merged review frontier and runs the review fan-in guard over the recorded partition. Each fan-out CLI lineage keeps its coverage graph in `graph-db/` inside its own directory: `fanout-run.cjs` exports `DEEP_LOOP_COVERAGE_DB_DIR` to it, so the graph steps stay inside the lineage write surface instead of the shared runtime database.

79:### Frontier Node Store (expansive depth only)
80-
81:`research/frontier-nodes.jsonl` is the node-status store for a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive`. A default run never creates it. Rows are append-only and carry `{nodeId, parentId, depth, scope, question, status, reason, iteration, dedupeKey}`. The last row for a node wins, so a status change is one more row rather than an edit. `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs` is the only writer.
82-
83:Statuses are `open`, `active`, `answered`, `blocked`, `exhausted`, `abstained` and `pruned`. A frontier question resolves when its node reaches a terminal status.
84-
85:The reducer projects the store as `registry.frontier {nodes, stats, budget}`, applies the depth budget so an unspent share rolls down to the next depth only, closes a spent band's open nodes as `exhausted` with reason `budget`, and adds a `## 6B. FRONTIER` dashboard section.
86-
87----
[0m$ [0mrg -n --no-config -C 2 "expansive|frontier|depth-mode|depthMode|maxFrontier|budgetSplit|50/35|derived budget|max-iterations" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md | head -160
142-The extended row carries `{"type":"event","event":"extended","sessionId","generation","lineageMode":"completed-continue","continuedFromRun","extension","timestamp"}`, where `extension` holds `addedIterations`, `priorMaxIterations`, `newMaxIterations`, `priorMaxDurationMinutes`, `newMaxDurationMinutes` and `elapsedMinutesAtExtension`. It has no `archivedPath` or `parentSessionId`, so the field rule below covers `resumed` and `restarted` only.
143-
144:**Convergence window.** Under `stopPolicy: convergence`, an extension with nothing new to cover re-stops on `all_questions_answered` at the first convergence check. The extension step prints a warning when this is likely; to keep the run going, pass `--convergence-mode=off`, use `stopPolicy: max-iterations`, or add questions to `research/inbox.jsonl`.
145-
146-**Consecutive failures.** A run that completed on consecutive failures re-stops at the consecutive-failure check even under `--convergence-mode=off`, because that check runs before the `off` branch and the trailing failed iterations are still in the log.
--
180-- Three consecutive failed iterations? STOP with stop reason `error`
181-- Stuck count >= threshold? STUCK_RECOVERY
182:- All questions answered, or average newInfoRatio < threshold? STOP candidate. Below the `minIterations` floor it becomes CONTINUE, and so does a `--depth-mode=expansive` run while a frontier node is open or active. Otherwise it stops the loop only when the stop policy allows a convergence stop and the quality gates pass
183-- Otherwise: CONTINUE
184-

409-### Expansive Depth Frontier (opt-in)
410-
411:Under `--depth-mode=expansive` the loop keeps a frontier of follow-up questions beside the key questions seeded at init. The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode: "expansive"` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 per depth, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations` unless the config sets a number. The depth-1 seed answers only to the root-children cap, but every seed that is not pruned counts toward the run cap. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(10, 2 × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes, 16 at the full six seeds, and the seed step prints the derived budget. The node cap stays half that budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. A run without the flag keeps the default path and its behavior. The one byte change is the expansive-only persona paragraph in the CLI lineage prompt, which the golden test strips before it compares.
412-
413:`step_frontier_seed` decomposes the topic into 3 to 6 depth-1 nodes before iteration 1. On a plain run it sits in `phase_init` after `step_create_config`; on a fan-out run it runs before `step_fanout_spawn`. It calls `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs seed`.
414-
415:A leaf may append `frontier_proposal` rows to its delta only when its prompt carries a FRONTIER block, which `frontier-admit.cjs block` computes from the node's remaining child slots and the prompt pack renders through `{frontier_block}`. `step_frontier_admit` runs before `step_reduce_state` and calls `frontier-admit.cjs admit`, which applies six checks in order: relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget, cycle. The first failure prunes the proposal with that reason. Admitted nodes become `research/inbox.jsonl` rows with `origin: "frontier"`.
416-
417-Next Focus picks the shallowest open node first, then the least visited, then the oldest, and writes `Frontier node <id> (depth N, scope S): <question>` so a visit does not read as an answer.
418-
419:Under stuck recovery the handler closes the focused node as `exhausted` with reason `stuck` through `frontier-admit.cjs close`, keeps the `stuckRecovery` event, and moves to the next open node. Global recovery runs only when no node is open.
420-
421:On a fan-out run, `fanout-run.cjs --depth-mode expansive` deals the seeded open depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order across lineages, writes each lineage's `frontier-nodes.jsonl` and inbox rows before it spawns, binds `frontier_scope:` in the lineage prompt and records `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json`. `fanout-merge.cjs` merges the lineage frontiers, each node keeping its lineage label, and records a partition guard that names any dealt node no lineage reports.
422-
423-### Ideas Backlog Convention

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md:238:- The flag writes `antiConvergence.depthMode` and the caps in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6 (the review root seed takes up to 8 depth-1 sub-targets, against 6 for research), a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations` unless the config sets a number. The depth-1 seed answers only to the root-children cap, but every seed that is not pruned counts toward the run cap. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(7, 2 × D × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes and D configured dimensions, and the seed step prints the derived budget. With the four canonical dimensions the ceiling of 24 binds from two seeds on, so one run cannot fund every node at every depth of a wide review; split a wide review with fan-out, where each lineage sizes its own budget from its share of the seed. A lineage dealt no seed node, or a run whose seeds were all pruned, runs at its loop default instead: 12 iterations for a native lineage and 7 for a CLI one or a single run. The node cap stays half the budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. At about seven to eight minutes an iteration, a 24-iteration review can reach `maxDurationMinutes` (120) before its iteration cap. `antiConvergence.depthMode` is locked against the optimizer; the `expansiveDepth` caps are in neither its tunable nor its locked list.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md:240:- `step_frontier_admit` runs before the reducer and prunes a `frontier_proposal` on the first failed check among relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, written only by `frontier-admit.cjs`; review has no inbox.
.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md:158:`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, which no default run creates, and an open node is filed as open search debt, so the run spends the iteration that node still needs. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. Once every node is closed and at least one was answered, a run that forms no other stop candidate gets `frontier_drained`, which the legal-stop gates still decide, so a run whose frontier work is done stops before its cap. The caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations`. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(7, 2 × D × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes and D configured dimensions, and the seed step prints the derived budget. With the four canonical dimensions the ceiling of 24 binds from two seeds on, so one run cannot fund every node at every depth of a wide review. Split a wide review with fan-out, where each lineage sizes its own budget from its share of the seed. A lineage dealt no seed node, or a run whose seeds were all pruned, runs at its loop default instead: 12 iterations for a native lineage and 7 for a CLI one or a single run. The node cap stays half the budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. At about seven to eight minutes an iteration, a 24-iteration review can reach `maxDurationMinutes` (120) before its iteration cap.

  }
  // Insert before the existing heading, preserving its authored text verbatim.

// A free-text leaf focus must not hide a required dimension the run has not covered yet.
function pickReviewBaseFocus(iterationFiles, registry) {
  const leafFocus = iterationFiles.map((iteration) => iteration.nextFocus).filter(Boolean).at(-1) || '';
  const uncovered = Object.keys(registry.dimensionCoverage).filter((dimension) => !registry.dimensionCoverage[dimension]);
  const leafKey = String(leafFocus).toLowerCase().trim();
  if (leafFocus && (uncovered.length === 0 || uncovered.some((dimension) => leafKey.includes(dimension)))) {
    return leafFocus;
  }
  return uncovered[0] || leafFocus || '[All dimensions covered]';
}

// The expansive frontier is the run's own queue, and the node it chose carries
// the dimension that node still owes, so a leaf's free-text focus cannot point
// the next iteration at work the frontier has already closed or reordered.
function pickReviewFrontierFocus(frontier) {
  if (!frontier || typeof frontier !== 'object' || !frontier.activeNodeId) return '';
  const nodes = Array.isArray(frontier.nodes) ? frontier.nodes : [];
  const node = nodes.find((candidate) => candidate && candidate.nodeId === frontier.activeNodeId);
  if (!node) return '';
  const scope = Array.isArray(node.scope) ? node.scope.join(', ') : (node.scope || 'unscoped');
  return `Frontier node ${node.nodeId} (depth ${node.depth}, scope ${scope}): ${node.question} - review the ${frontier.activeNextDimension} dimension`;
}

function updateStrategyContent(strategyContent, registry, iterationFiles, options = {}, iterationRecords = []) {
  // Early return when there is no strategy file to update. Empty content
  // cannot contain the machine-owned anchors and replaceAnchorSection would
  // correctly fail-close — but for that specific case we intentionally skip
  // rather than throw so bootstrap flows (no strategy yet) still work.
  if (!strategyContent) {
    return strategyContent;
  }

  const anchorOptions = { createMissing: Boolean(options.createMissingAnchors) };
  const severity = registry.findingsBySeverity;
  const runningFindings = [
    `- P0 (Blockers): ${severity.P0}`,
    `- P1 (Required): ${severity.P1}`,
    `- P2 (Suggestions): ${severity.P2}`,
    `- Resolved: ${registry.resolvedFindingsCount}`,
  ].join('\n');

  const completedDimensions = Object.keys(registry.dimensionCoverage)
    .filter((dimension) => registry.dimensionCoverage[dimension])
    .map((dimension) => `- [x] ${dimension}`)
    .join('\n') || '[None yet]';

  const remainingDimensions = Object.keys(registry.dimensionCoverage)
    .filter((dimension) => !registry.dimensionCoverage[dimension])
    .map((dimension) => `- [ ] ${dimension}`)
    .join('\n') || '[All dimensions complete]';

  // Default next-focus: a leaf focus naming an uncovered dimension, else the first uncovered dimension, else the leaf focus.
  let nextFocus = pickReviewBaseFocus(iterationFiles, registry);
  // An open node outranks the leaf's own focus; the pivot override and the
  // blocked-stop recovery below still outrank the node, because a saturated
  // direction or a hard block is what the run must heed before spending depth.
  const frontierFocus = pickReviewFrontierFocus(registry.frontier);
  if (frontierFocus) nextFocus = frontierFocus;
  if (registry.divergence.currentFocus) {
    nextFocus = registry.divergence.currentFocus;
  }

  // Prefer the latest blocked-stop
  // recovery only when blocked-stop is genuinely the most recent loop event.
  // The earlier implementation read timestamps from the markdown iteration
  // files, but parseIterationFile() never captures timestamps, so
  // latestIterationTimestamp was always undefined and any historical
  // blocked-stop permanently pinned next-focus. Fix: source recency from the
  // JSONL iteration records (which do carry timestamps) instead.
  const latestBlockedStop = registry.blockedStopHistory?.at(-1);
  if (latestBlockedStop && latestBlockedStop.timestamp) {
    const latestIterationTimestamp = iterationRecords
      .filter((record) => record?.type === 'iteration' && typeof record.timestamp === 'string')
      .map((record) => record.timestamp)
      .at(-1);
    const blockedIsMostRecent = !latestIterationTimestamp
      || latestBlockedStop.timestamp >= latestIterationTimestamp;
    if (blockedIsMostRecent) {
      const blockers = formatBlockedByList(latestBlockedStop.blockedBy);
      const recovery = latestBlockedStop.recoveryStrategy || '[no recovery strategy provided]';
      nextFocus = [
        `BLOCKED on: ${blockers}`,
        `Recovery: ${recovery}`,
        'Address the blocking gates before the next iteration.',
      ].join('\n');
    }
  }

  let updated = strategyContent;
  updated = replaceAnchorSection(updated, 'review-dimensions', '3. REVIEW DIMENSIONS (remaining)', remainingDimensions, anchorOptions);
  updated = replaceAnchorSection(updated, 'completed-dimensions', '4. COMPLETED DIMENSIONS', completedDimensions, anchorOptions);
  updated = replaceAnchorSection(updated, 'running-findings', '5. RUNNING FINDINGS', runningFindings, anchorOptions);
  updated = replaceAnchorSection(
    updated,
    'exhausted-approaches',
[0m$ [0msed -n '2590,2680p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/scripts/reduce-state.cjs
const continuityFocus = deriveNextFocusFromContinuity({
    iterationFiles: filteredInputs.iterationFiles,
    iterationRecords: filteredInputs.iterationRecords,
    carriedForwardOpenQuestions: filteredInputs.carriedForwardOpenQuestions,
    machineOpenQuestions: filteredInputs.openQuestions,
    leafNextFocus: latestLeafNextFocus,
    resolvedQuestions: Array.isArray(registry.resolvedQuestions) ? registry.resolvedQuestions : [],
  });

  const normalizedFocus = normalizeOptionalText(continuityFocus).toLowerCase();
  const shouldUseIdeaFocus = !normalizedFocus
    || normalizedFocus === '[none yet]'
    || normalizedFocus === '[all tracked questions are resolved]';
  if (!shouldUseIdeaFocus) {
    return continuityFocus;
  }

  const promotedIdea = Array.isArray(registry.promotedIdeas)
    ? registry.promotedIdeas[0]
    : null;
  return promotedIdea?.idea || continuityFocus;
}

function buildRegistry(strategyQuestions, iterationFiles, iterationRecords, eventRecords, reducerState = {}) {
  const questionCoverageRecords = iterationRecords.filter((record) => {
    const status = normalizeText(String(record.status || 'complete')).toLowerCase();
    return !['error', 'failed', 'failure', 'stuck', 'thought'].includes(status);
  });

  const resolutionRecords = strategyQuestions.map((question) => {
    const normalized = normalizeText(question.text);
    return questionCoverageRecords.find((record) => {
      const answeredQuestions = Array.isArray(record.answeredQuestions)
        ? record.answeredQuestions.map(normalizeText)
        : [];
      const keyQuestions = Array.isArray(record.keyQuestions)
        ? record.keyQuestions.map(normalizeText)
        : [];
      return answeredQuestions.includes(normalized)
        || (answeredQuestions.length > 0 && keyQuestions.length === 1 && keyQuestions[0] === normalized)
        || normalizeOptionalText(record.focus) === normalized;
    });
  });

  // The budget runs after the iteration evidence, so a node whose question is
  // already answered is never closed as budget-exhausted.
  const frontierState = reducerState.frontier
    ? applyFrontierBudget(
      reducerState.frontier.nodes,
      iterationRecords,
      reducerState.frontier.maxIterations,
      reducerState.frontier.budgetSplit,
      new Set(strategyQuestions
        .filter((question, index) => question.inboxId && (question.checked || resolutionRecords[index]))
        .map((question) => normalizeQuestionId(question.inboxId))),
    )
    : null;

  // A frontier node that closed without an answer (exhausted, blocked, abstained,
  // pruned) or one the store marks answered stops counting as an open question.
  const FRONTIER_TERMINAL = new Set(['answered', 'blocked', 'exhausted', 'abstained', 'pruned']);
  const frontierCloseByNode = new Map(
    (frontierState ? frontierState.nodes : [])
      .filter((node) => FRONTIER_TERMINAL.has(node.status))
      .map((node) => [node.nodeId, node]),
  );

  const keyedQuestions = strategyQuestions.map((question, index) => {
    const normalized = normalizeText(question.text);
    const resolutionRecord = resolutionRecords[index];
    const frontierClose = question.inboxId ? frontierCloseByNode.get(normalizeQuestionId(question.inboxId)) : undefined;
    const resolved = question.checked || Boolean(resolutionRecord) || Boolean(frontierClose);
    const id = normalizeQuestionId(question.id);
    const promotedQuestionId = normalizeQuestionId(question.promotedQuestionId);
    const inboxId = normalizeQuestionId(question.inboxId);
    const injectedAtIteration = normalizeQuestionIteration(question.injectedAtIteration);
    const operatorDecision = normalizeQuestionDecision(question.operatorDecision);
    const conflictId = normalizeQuestionId(question.conflictId);
    return {
      id: id || promotedQuestionId || `question-${index + 1}-${slugify(normalized)}`,
      inboxId,
      text: normalized,
      origin: normalizeQuestionOrigin(question.origin),
      source: normalizeQuestionSource(question.source, LEGACY_IMPORT_SOURCE),
      injectedAtIteration,
      promotedQuestionId,
      ...(operatorDecision ? { operatorDecision } : {}),

### DRV-017 | P0 override blocks convergence

#### Description
Verify new P0 finding sets newFindingsRatio >= 0.50, blocking convergence.

#### Scenario Contract
Prompt: `Validate that new P0 findings block deep-review convergence by forcing a high newFindingsRatio.`

Expected signals: P0 finding sets `newFindingsRatio >= 0.50`, this blocks the rolling average signal from contributing to convergence, the composite score cannot reach 0.60, and review continues.

#### Test Execution
> **Feature File:** [DRV-017](../manual-testing-playbook/convergence-and-recovery/p0-override-blocks-convergence.md)

#!/usr/bin/env node

// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MODULE: Deep-Loop Terminal Gate Results
// ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// A review that stops records three terminal gates: the cited evidence behind
// its findings, whether those findings sit inside the reviewed scope, and the
// dimension coverage behind its stop. Judged from prose, a gate passes on the
// writer's word; computed here from loop state, the same state always reports
// the same three verdicts. Each row carries a digest of the summary it was
// decided from, so a later reader can tell two passes on the same state from
// two passes on different state without keeping the state itself.

'use strict';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 1. IMPORTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const { createHash } = require('node:crypto');
const fs = require('node:fs');

const {
  assertKnownFlags,
} = require('./lib/cli-guards.cjs');

const {
  buildStopDecisionInput,
  evaluateReviewLegalStopGates,
  readJsonObjectFile,
  readStateLogRows,
} = require('./convergence.cjs');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 2. CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const EVIDENCE_GATE_ID = 'evidence';
const SCOPE_GATE_ID = 'scope';
const COVERAGE_GATE_ID = 'coverage';
// Every key this entrypoint reads from the parsed arguments. A flag missing
// here is treated as an unknown option, so this list is the accepted set.
const TERMINAL_GATE_FLAGS = ['configPath', 'registryPath', 'stateLog'];
// A gate result is a report, not a verdict on the run: a failed row changes no
// state, so an unreadable input is the only condition that stops the script.
const EXIT_ERROR = 2;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// 3. HELPERS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function inputError(message) {
  const err = new Error(message);
  err.code = 'INPUT_VALIDATION';
  return err;
}

function isPlainObject(value) {
  return Boolean(value) && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function numberOrZero(value) {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
}

// A scope entry and a finding file reach this script from different machines,
// so both are compared in one spelling: a checkout's separator and its
// relative prefix must not decide whether a finding is inside the scope.
function normalizePath(value) {
  return value.replace(/\\/gu, '/').replace(/^\.\//u, '');
}

// The config records scope as a list, or as named lists once a review splits
// its target by kind; both spellings name the same reviewed paths.
function scopeEntries(reviewScopeFiles) {
  const entries = [];
  const collect = (values) => {
    for (const value of values) {
      if (typeof value !== 'string' || value === '') continue;
