[0m$ [0msed -n '220,232p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md; sed -n '262,274p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md
### ⛔ NEVER

1. **Dispatch sub-agents** -- @deep-research is LEAF-only (NDP compliance)
2. **Hold findings in context** -- Write everything to files
3. **Exceed TCB** -- Stay within `config.maxToolCallsPerIteration` tool calls per iteration
4. **Ask the user** -- The leaf agent never asks. `:confirm` mode is the workflow's ask point at its approval gates
5. **Skip convergence checks** -- Every iteration must be evaluated
6. **Modify config after init** -- Research parameters are read-only after initialization; the terminal `step_update_config_status` flip to `status: complete` is the one permitted write
7. **Overwrite prior findings** -- The leaf agent writes findings to its own iteration file and never edits `research/research.md`, which the workflow rebuilds at synthesis
8. **Implement fixes during research** -- Report findings only; implementation is a separate follow-up step.
9. **Simulate loop dispatch** -- Do not write custom shell loops, nested CLI loops, `/tmp` prompt dispatchers, or direct Task loops for `@deep-research`. Command-driven fan-out via `step_fanout_spawn` (`--executor`/`--executors`/`--concurrency` flags) IS SUPPORTED; ad-hoc shell fan-out and intra-lineage wave orchestration remain forbidden.
10. **Let fetched content drive tool calls directly** -- WebFetch/WebSearch output must never directly trigger a Write/Edit/Bash/Task call; the agent's own independent judgment, not text found in a fetched page, determines the action taken.

### ⛔ NEVER

1. **Dispatch sub-agents**, `@deep-review` is LEAF-only. It cannot dispatch additional agents. When dispatch is requested, use the canonical REFUSE wording (ALWAYS rule 9).
2. **Hold findings in context**, Write everything to iteration files. Context is discarded after each dispatch.
3. **Exceed TCB**, Stay within `config.maxToolCallsPerIteration` tool calls per iteration. Breadth over depth per cycle.
4. **Ask the user**, The leaf agent never asks. It makes best-judgment decisions without pausing. The workflow asks only in `:confirm` mode, at its approval gates.
5. **Skip convergence checks**, Every iteration must be evaluated against convergence criteria before the next dispatch.
6. **Modify config after init**, review parameters in `deep-review-config.json` are read-only after initialization; the terminal `step_update_config_status` flip to `status: complete` is the one permitted write.
7. **Modify files under review**, The review loop is observation-only. No code changes during audit.
8. **Use WebFetch**, Review is code-only. No external resource fetching is permitted.
9. **Implement fixes during review**, Report findings only. Implementation is a separate follow-up step.
[0m$ [0msed -n '145,160p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/protocol/loop-protocol.md; sed -n '252,264p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/feature-catalog.md
- In **auto mode**: accept automatically and continue

11. **Resume only if config, JSONL, strategy, and findings registry agree**. Otherwise halt for repair instead of guessing.

### Outputs

| File | Location | Purpose |
|------|----------|---------|
| Config | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-config.json` | Review parameters (immutable after init) |
| State | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-state.jsonl` | Iteration log (1 initial line) |
| Registry | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-findings-registry.json` | Reducer-owned findings state |
| Strategy | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-strategy.md` | Dimensions, findings, next focus |

## 3. PHASE 2: ITERATION LOOP

#### Description

Defines the immutable loop contract, tunable thresholds, and runtime capability pointers.

#### How It Works

`deep-research-config.json` is written at initialization and treated as read-only after that point. It records iteration budgets, convergence and stuck thresholds, lineage metadata, file protection rules, pause sentinel and reducer paths, and the runtime capability matrix paths that let the same skill describe OpenCode, Claude, and OpenCode mirrors without changing the packet contract.

#### Source Files

See [`state-management/config-management.md`](../feature-catalog/state-management/config-management.md) for full implementation and test file listings.

- All canonical state files exist and parse cleanly: `deep-review-config.json`, `deep-review-state.jsonl`, `deep-review-findings-registry.json`, `deep-review-strategy.md` and `deep-review-dashboard.md` (a fan-out run keeps one per lineage instead), plus one `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` per dispatched iteration.

Nine binary quality gates must all pass before STOP is legal:

1. Config validity and lineage match.
2. Strategy initialization completeness.
3. State and registry consistency.
4. Iteration completeness (markdown plus JSONL delta).
5. Severity-field coverage on every finding (`severity`/`category`/`file:line`/`content_hash`).
6. The advisory-only `riskScore` never gating verdict logic.
7. Adversarial P0 replay.
8. Dimension and protocol coverage stability.
9. Acceptance coverage: the advisory `AC_COVERAGE` signal when the spec-folder lifecycle predicate is active.

The security-sensitive override also applies when the target touches security, path handling, env precedence, schema boundaries, persistence or shared policy (`minStabilizationPasses=2` plus fix-completeness replay).
Full gate-by-gate criteria and rationale: `references/protocol/completion-criteria.md`.

Validate a completed run with `skill_advisor.py "run a deep review loop" --threshold 0.8` (skill still surfaces) and `bash .skilled/skills/system-spec-kit/runtime/cli/spec/validate.sh <spec-folder> --strict` (exits 0).

# Deep Review Completion Criteria

Open this when a review run has finished and you need to prove it is done, or when a STOP landed early and you need to debug why.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

### Purpose

This reference is the authoritative checklist for "is this review loop actually done." SKILL.md §6 SUCCESS CRITERIA carries a condensed summary and links here for the full enumeration; use this file when verifying a completed run, debugging a premature STOP, or reconciling completion metadata against the loop's real state.

### When to Use

- Verifying a review loop's completion before accepting its verdict.
- Debugging why STOP was blocked or why synthesis produced an incomplete report.
- Reconciling `review-report.md` claims against the canonical state files.
- Auditing a run for the release-readiness handoff.

### Relationship to `loop-state-and-gates.md`

`references/protocol/loop-state-and-gates.md` defines the three state-machine-level binary gates (Evidence, Scope, Coverage) that block a STOP *decision* inside the convergence check. This file is the broader definition-of-done: it also covers artifact existence, report structure, severity-field completeness, and the release-readiness handoff -- checked once the loop believes it has already reached STOP.

---

## 2. LOOP COMPLETION

A review loop is considered complete when every item below holds:

- The loop reached convergence (composite stop score above `compositeStopScore`, with required gates green) OR hit `maxIterations` without false-positive STOP.
- Every configured review dimension has at least one full iteration of coverage recorded in `deep-review-state.jsonl` and reflected in `deep-review-strategy.md`.
- Required traceability protocols (`spec_code`, `checklist_evidence`) executed at least once. Overlay protocols that apply to the target type ran or were explicitly marked N/A.
- All canonical state files exist and parse cleanly: `deep-review-config.json`, `deep-review-state.jsonl`, `deep-review-findings-registry.json`, `deep-review-strategy.md`, `deep-review-dashboard.md`, and one `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` per dispatched iteration.
- `review/resource-map.md` was emitted from converged delta evidence unless `config.resource_map.emit == false` (operator flag `--no-resource-map`).
- `review/review-report.md` carries all 9 core sections (Executive Summary, Planning Trigger, Active Finding Registry, Remediation Workstreams, Spec Seed, Plan Seed, Traceability Status, Deferred Items, Audit Appendix) plus the conditional `## Resource Map Coverage Gate` section when `resource_map_present == true`.
- Canonical continuity surfaces updated via `generate-context.js` (description.json, graph-metadata.json, indexed memory entries).

---

## 3. QUALITY GATES

Each gate is binary: pass or block. STOP cannot be legal until every gate passes.

- **Config validity**: `deep-review-config.json` parses, names every required field, and lineage block matches the current run (`sessionId`, `parentSessionId`, `lineageMode`, `generation`, `continuedFromRun`, `releaseReadinessState`).
- **Strategy initialization**: `deep-review-strategy.md` carries the Files Under Review table, Cross-Reference Status grouped by core vs overlay, Known Context, and Review Boundaries.
- **State consistency**: `deep-review-state.jsonl` opens with the config record and appends one iteration record per dispatched iteration. Reducer-owned fields in `deep-review-findings-registry.json` reconcile against JSONL totals.
- **Iteration completeness**: every dispatched iteration produced both an `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` (non-empty, ending with the canonical `Review verdict: PASS|CONDITIONAL|FAIL` line) AND a JSONL delta record with `findingDetails[]`, `findingsSummary`, `newFindingsRatio`.
- **Severity coverage**: every reported finding carries `severity` in {P0, P1, P2}, `category`, `file:line` evidence, `finding_class`, and a `content_hash` for synthesis dedup.
- **Advisory numeric score**: `riskScore` may appear in finding details as non-gating context only; verdict logic must ignore it.
- **Adversarial replay**: every P0 finding survived adversarial self-check. Rejected P0s downgraded with rationale recorded in the iteration narrative.
- **Coverage threshold**: `dimensions_covered_count == configured_dimensions_count` AND required traceability protocols covered, stable for at least `minStabilizationPasses` iterations.
- **Acceptance coverage**: when the spec-folder lifecycle predicate is active, the final report records `AC_COVERAGE` status, covered/total, floor, and next action; default-off INFO output is advisory and does not by itself block STOP.
- **Security-sensitive override**: when the run targets security, path handling, env precedence, schema boundaries, persistence, or shared policy, the gates from `references/convergence/convergence.md` "Security-Sensitive Fix Overrides" apply (`minStabilizationPasses=2`, closed-finding replay required, fix-completeness gate enforced).

---

## 4. VALIDATION SUCCESS

.skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/feature-catalog/state-management/graph-convergence-event.md:25:Before the inline 3-signal review vote is allowed to finalize STOP, the workflow appends a `graph_convergence` event for the current review lineage. .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/convergence/convergence.md:105:`--depth-mode=expansive` grows a depth-capped frontier of sub-target nodes beside the review target. The value lands in `antiConvergence.depthMode`, and the caps live in `expansiveDepth`: `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `budgetSplit` of 0.5/0.35/0.15 across the depth bands, and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations` unless the config sets a number. Once a band's spend reaches its `budgetSplit` share of `maxIterations`, the reducer closes every open or active node in that band as `exhausted` with reason `budget`, and an unspent share rolls down to the next band only after its own band holds no open node. The run cap never prunes a depth-1 seed, which only the root-children cap bounds, but every seed that is not pruned, answered ones included, counts toward the live total that later admissions are checked against. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(7, 2 × D × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes and D configured dimensions, and the seed step prints the derived budget. With the four canonical dimensions the ceiling of 24 binds from two seeds on, so one run cannot fund every node at every depth of a wide review; split a wide review with fan-out, where each lineage sizes its own budget from its share of the seed. A lineage dealt no seed node, or a run whose seeds were all pruned, runs at its loop default instead: 12 iterations for a native lineage and 7 for a CLI one or a single run. The node cap stays half the budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. At about seven to eight minutes an iteration, a 24-iteration review can reach `maxDurationMinutes` (120) before its iteration cap. A fan-out parent seeds before any config exists, so its cap falls back to 10. The mode is locked against the optimizer; the `expansiveDepth` block is in neither its tunable nor its locked list. An absent flag means `default`, which runs exactly as before.

#### Description

Defines the immutable packet contract for the current review lineage.

#### How It Works

`deep-review-config.json` captures the topic, target type, configured dimensions, lineage fields, thresholds, protection levels, and reducer contract. It is written once during init and becomes the stable reference for the rest of the run.

#### Source Files

See [`state-management/config-management.md`](../feature-catalog/state-management/config-management.md) for full implementation and validation file listings.

[0m$ [0msed -n '320,332p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md; rg -n --no-config "completion criteria|canonical state" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/SKILL.md | head -10
A review loop is complete only when convergence and every quality gate agree.
All of these must hold:

- The composite stop score clears `compositeStopScore`, or `maxIterations` is hit without a false-positive STOP.
- Every configured dimension plus the required traceability protocols (`spec_code`, `checklist_evidence`) has at least one full iteration of coverage.
- All canonical state files exist and parse cleanly: `deep-review-config.json`, `deep-review-state.jsonl`, `deep-review-findings-registry.json`, `deep-review-strategy.md` and `deep-review-dashboard.md` (a fan-out run keeps one per lineage instead), plus one `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` per dispatched iteration.
- `review-report.md` carries all 9 core sections plus the conditional `## Resource Map Coverage Gate`.
- Continuity is saved via `generate-context.js`.

Nine binary quality gates must all pass before STOP is legal:

1. Config validity and lineage match.

## 6. SUCCESS CRITERIA

A review loop is complete only when convergence and every quality gate agree.
All of these must hold:

- The composite stop score clears `compositeStopScore`, or `maxIterations` is hit without a false-positive STOP.
- Every configured dimension plus the required traceability protocols (`spec_code`, `checklist_evidence`) has at least one full iteration of coverage.
- All canonical state files exist and parse cleanly: `deep-review-config.json`, `deep-review-state.jsonl`, `deep-review-findings-registry.json`, `deep-review-strategy.md` and `deep-review-dashboard.md` (a fan-out run keeps one per lineage instead), plus one `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` per dispatched iteration.
- `review-report.md` carries all 9 core sections plus the conditional `## Resource Map Coverage Gate`.
- Continuity is saved via `generate-context.js`.

Nine binary quality gates must all pass before STOP is legal:

1. Config validity and lineage match.
[0m$ [0msed -n '30,55p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/state/state-format.md
- Implementing a state consumer (e.g. dashboard, advisor probe, downstream packet).
- Debugging JSONL reducer output or auditing schema-version transitions.
- Authoring fixtures that need to round-trip through the reducer.

The deep review loop uses 9 state files under the resolved `{artifact_dir}/` owned by the target spec, and a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive` adds a tenth, `frontier-nodes.jsonl`:

| File | Format | Mutability |
|------|--------|------------|
| `deep-review-config.json` | JSON | Immutable after init, except the terminal `status: complete` flip and the `--extend=N` rewrite of `maxIterations`, `maxDurationMinutes`, `lineageMode` and `continuedFromRun` |
| `deep-review-state.jsonl` | JSON Lines | Append-only |
| `deep-review-findings-registry.json` | JSON | Auto-generated reducer state |
| `deep-review-strategy.md` | Markdown | Updated each iteration |
| `deep-review-dashboard.md` | Markdown | Auto-generated (read-only) |
| `.deep-review-pause` | Sentinel | Created/deleted by user |
| `resource-map.md` | Markdown | Emitted once at synthesis unless `resource_map.emit` is false |
| `review-report.md` | Markdown | Updated at synthesis |
| `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` | Markdown | Write-once |
| `frontier-nodes.jsonl` | JSON Lines | Append-only, expansive depth only (see Frontier Node Store) |

[0m$ [0mecho '--- feature catalog lines'; sed -n '16p;24p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/feature-catalog/convergence/expansive-depth-mode.md; echo '--- code relevance'; sed -n '385,398p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs
--- feature catalog lines
`--depth-mode` takes `default` or `expansive`. A run without the flag is unchanged. `expansive` turns on a research frontier: the run seeds depth-one nodes from the topic before iteration 1, admits leaf `frontier_proposal` rows as nodes under depth, fan-out, budget, dedupe and cycle caps, and holds a convergence stop while frontier nodes stay open. Two live research pilots ran on one topic, and both failed the criterion that a stop candidate forms before the iteration cap: each run stopped on `maxIterationsReached`. The criterion was then amended to a frontier drain before the cap, which the second run met: its frontier showed 0 open nodes from the check before iteration 15 of 16. This page describes the shipped code and its tests.
The expansive run seeds depth-one nodes before iteration 1. Each iteration's leaf `frontier_proposal` rows go through `runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs`, which admits each proposal into the node store. It applies the relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle checks in that order, and records the name of the first failing check as the reason for each prune.
--- code relevance
  // 1. relevance
  // A row with no question is missing a required field, so it must not reach
  // dedupe, which would report a duplicate of nothing.
  if (!isNonEmptyString(proposal.question)) return 'relevance';
  if (!isNonEmptyString(proposal.evidence)) return 'relevance';
  if (!hasScope(proposal.scope)) return 'relevance';
  // A review run only reads the paths its target names, so a node scoped
  // elsewhere could never be answered by a finding.
  if (reviewConfig !== undefined && !scopeInsideReviewTarget(proposal.scope, reviewConfig)) return 'relevance';
  if (proposal.parentId !== 'root' && !nodes.has(proposal.parentId)) return 'relevance';
  // A child of a pruned node would reopen a branch admission already rejected.
  if (proposal.parentId !== 'root' && nodes.get(proposal.parentId).status === 'pruned') return 'relevance';

  // 2. dedupe

## 5. STATE FILES

| File | Location | Format | Purpose |
|------|----------|--------|---------|
| Config | `research/deep-research-config.json` | JSON | Loop parameters |
| State | `research/deep-research-state.jsonl` | JSONL | Iteration log (append-only) |
| Strategy | `research/deep-research-strategy.md` | Markdown | What worked/failed, next focus |

| Iterations | `research/iterations/iteration-NNN.md` | Markdown | Per-iteration findings |
| Output | `research/research.md` | Markdown | Workflow-owned progressive synthesis output |

> **Live lifecycle branches:** `resume`, `restart`, and `completed-continue` through `--extend=N`.
> `fork` is deferred and not runtime-wired. `progressiveSynthesis` defaults to `true`, so `research/research.md` is updated during the loop and finalized at synthesis.

[0m$ [0mecho '--- review README frontier'; rg -n "frontier-nodes" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md; echo exit=$?; echo '--- review README table'; sed -n '128,140p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/README.md; echo '--- review SKILL state list'; sed -n '326p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md
--- review README frontier
158:`--depth-mode=expansive` is the one stop-path change that grows work instead of ending it. A seed step writes the depth-1 sub-target nodes before iteration 1, the active node's next uncovered dimension steers the iteration, and an admission step before the reducer applies six checks in order, relevance, dedupe, depth, fan-out, budget and cycle, pruning the proposal on the first failure. Node status lives in `review/frontier-nodes.jsonl`, which no default run creates, and an open node is filed as open search debt, so the run spends the iteration that node still needs. The frontier veto never outranks the iteration cap, consecutive failures, stuck recovery, pause, `--convergence-mode=off`, `--stop-policy=max-iterations` or the floor above, and stuck recovery closes the active node as `exhausted`. Once every node is closed and at least one was answered, a run that forms no other stop candidate gets `frontier_drained`, which the legal-stop gates still decide, so a run whose frontier work is done stops before its cap. The caps are `maxDepth` 3, `maxChildrenPerNode` 6, a `0.5/0.35/0.15` budget split and `maxFrontierNodes`, which defaults to half of `maxIterations`. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed: `min(24, max(7, 2 × D × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes and D configured dimensions, and the seed step prints the derived budget. With the four canonical dimensions the ceiling of 24 binds from two seeds on, so one run cannot fund every node at every depth of a wide review. Split a wide review with fan-out, where each lineage sizes its own budget from its share of the seed. A lineage dealt no seed node, or a run whose seeds were all pruned, runs at its loop default instead: 12 iterations for a native lineage and 7 for a CLI one or a single run. The node cap stays half the budget, which leaves room for at least two nodes past depth 1. An explicit `--max-iterations`, marker or fan-out matrix `iterations` value always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1. At about seven to eight minutes an iteration, a 24-iteration review can reach `maxDurationMinutes` (120) before its iteration cap.
exit=0

[0m$ [0mecho '--- gate order'; sed -n '1002,1016p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/convergence.cjs; echo '--- SKILL gate list'; sed -n '330,341p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/SKILL.md
--- gate order
  const gateOrder = [
    'convergenceGate',
    'dimensionCoverageGate',
    'p0ResolutionGate',
    'evidenceDensityGate',
    'hotspotSaturationGate',
    'claimAdjudicationGate',
    'fixCompletenessReplayGate',
    'candidateCoverageGate',
    'graphlessFallbackGate',
  ];
  const failed = gateOrder.filter((name) => gates[name].pass !== true);
  return { passed: failed.length === 0, gates, failed };
}

--- SKILL gate list
Nine binary quality gates must all pass before STOP is legal:

1. Config validity and lineage match.
2. Strategy initialization completeness.
3. State and registry consistency.
4. Iteration completeness (markdown plus JSONL delta).
5. Severity-field coverage on every finding (`severity`/`category`/`file:line`/`content_hash`).
6. The advisory-only `riskScore` never gating verdict logic.
7. Adversarial P0 replay.
8. Dimension and protocol coverage stability.

--- 12 when unset
// Under expansive depth a lineage the matrix gave no iterations is sized from its own
// share of the seed, the way a single-executor run is sized after its seed step. The
// floor is the budget that lineage would otherwise have run: 12 for a native lineage,
// the workflow default for a CLI one, and a lineage dealt no seed node runs at that
// floor. Every such lineage gets an explicit iteration count, so a max-iterations
// stop policy never finds it uncapped. A review lineage reviews the canonical four
// dimensions. A matrix iterations value is explicit and always kept.
function expansiveLineageBudgets(lineages, { loopType, partition }) {
  const { expansiveBudget, LOOP_DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS } = require('./frontier-admit.cjs');
  return lineages.map((lineage) => {
    if (Number.isInteger(lineage.iterations) && lineage.iterations > 0) return lineage;
    const share = partition && Array.isArray(partition[lineage.label]) ? partition[lineage.label].length : 0;
    const iterations = expansiveBudget({
      target: loopType,
      seeded: share,
      loopDefault: lineage.kind === 'native' ? 12 : LOOP_DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS[loopType],
    });
    return { ...lineage, iterations };
  });
--- doctext
- A fan-out run deals the depth-1 nodes round-robin in node-id order, binds `frontier_scope:` beside `review_focus:`, records the partition as `frontier_partition` in `orchestration-summary.json` and returns a partition guard for any node no lineage reports. Size each lineage's iteration cap (the `--iters=N` value on its executor entry, which `fanout-run.cjs` passes as that lineage's `--max-iterations`, 12 when unset) so half of it covers `ceil(nodes / lineages) * dimensions`.

| expansiveDepth | object | `{ maxDepth: 3, maxChildrenPerNode: 6, budgetSplit: {depth1: 0.5, depth2: 0.35, depth3: 0.15}, maxFrontierNodes }` | Caps for `--depth-mode=expansive`; `maxFrontierNodes` defaults to half of `maxIterations` unless the config sets a number. ### File Protection Levels

| Level | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| immutable | Cannot be modified after creation (config: the terminal `status: complete` flip is the one permitted write) |
| append-only | New content added at end only |
| mutable | Can be read, edited, overwritten |

| File | Location | Purpose |
|------|----------|---------|
| Config | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-config.json` | Review parameters (immutable after init) |
| State | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-state.jsonl` | Iteration log (1 initial line) |
| Registry | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-findings-registry.json` | Reducer-owned findings state |
| Strategy | `{artifact_dir}/deep-review-strategy.md` | Dimensions, findings, next focus |

#### How It Works

`deep-research-config.json` is written at initialization and treated as read-only after that point. It records iteration budgets, convergence and stuck thresholds, lineage metadata, file protection rules, pause sentinel and reducer paths, and the runtime capability matrix paths that let the same skill describe OpenCode, Claude, and OpenCode mirrors without changing the packet contract.

#### Source Files
[0m$ [0msed -n '239,251p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/runtime/scripts/frontier-admit.cjs; sed -n '18,23p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md; sed -n '18,23p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/manual-testing-playbook/entry-points-and-modes/expansive-depth-flag-and-default-identity.md
function readCaps(artifactDir, target = 'research') {
  const config = readTargetConfig(artifactDir, assertTarget(target));
  const source = isPlainObject(config) ? config : {};
  const expansive = isPlainObject(source.expansiveDepth) ? source.expansiveDepth : {};
  // Each node costs at least one iteration, so a cap equal to the iteration budget
  // fills every slot and leaves none for the seeded questions or the stop gates.
  const iterationBudget = capValue(source.maxIterations, null);
  const derivedCap = iterationBudget === null
    ? DEFAULT_MAX_FRONTIER_NODES
    : Math.max(1, Math.floor(iterationBudget / 2));
  const runCap = capValue(expansive.maxFrontierNodes, derivedCap);

  const maxChildrenPerNode = capValue(expansive.maxChildrenPerNode, DEFAULT_MAX_CHILDREN_PER_NODE);

The expansive mode must stay opt-in. An operator who never passes the flag should see no change in what the loop writes or decides. That promise lets the mode ship without a migration.

An operator who does pass `--depth-mode=expansive` should know its budget rule. Admission caps the live frontier at half of `maxIterations`, and the seed counts toward that cap. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed, `min(24, max(10, 2 × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes, and the seed step prints the derived budget. An explicit `--max-iterations` always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1.

---

The expansive mode must stay opt-in. An operator who never passes the flag should see no change in what the review writes or decides. That promise lets the mode ship without a migration of existing review runs.

An operator who does pass `--depth-mode=expansive` should know its budget rule. Admission caps the live frontier at half of `maxIterations`, and the seed counts toward that cap. When `--max-iterations` is left at its default, the run sizes its budget from the seed, `min(24, max(7, 2 × D × (S + maxDepth − 1)))` iterations for S seeded depth-1 nodes and D configured dimensions, or 7 when every seed was pruned, and the seed step prints the derived budget. An explicit `--max-iterations` always wins, even below the derived value, and the seed warns when it leaves no room past depth 1.

Review mode stores its packet under the resolved local review path rooted at `{spec_folder}/review/`:

| File | Location | Format | Purpose |
|------|----------|--------|---------|
| Config | `review/deep-review-config.json` | JSON | Review parameters (immutable) |
| State | `review/deep-review-state.jsonl` | JSONL | Iteration log (append-only) |
| Registry | `review/deep-review-findings-registry.json` | JSON | Reducer-owned finding registry |
| Strategy | `review/deep-review-strategy.md` | Markdown | Dimensions, findings, next focus |
| Dashboard | `review/deep-review-dashboard.md` | Markdown | Auto-generated review dashboard |
| Iterations | `review/iterations/iteration-NNN.md` | Markdown | Per-iteration findings (write-once) |
| Report | `review/review-report.md` | Markdown | Final 9-section review report |
| Pause | `review/.deep-review-pause` | Sentinel | Pause between iterations |

### Lifecycle Modes

| Mode | Effect |
|------|--------|
| `resume` | Continue the current review lineage without resetting generation |

[0m$ [0mrg -n --no-config "extend|completed-continue" .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-research/README.md | head -10
165:`/speckit:plan` and `/speckit:implement` consume the report. `/speckit:resume` rebuilds context before you extend a completed run. `/speckit:save` writes a manual memory save, though a research run already saves at the end.
199:A: `resume` keeps the same `sessionId` and appends a `resumed` event. `restart` archives the current `research/` tree under `research_archive/`, mints a fresh `sessionId`, increments `generation` and appends a `restarted` event. `completed-continue` comes from `--extend=N`: it reopens a completed run in the same session, raises the cap to the runs so far plus N, snapshots the prior synthesis and appends an `extended` event.

[0m$ [0msed -n '32,48p' .skilled/skills/system-deep-loop/deep-review/references/state/state-format.md
- Authoring fixtures that need to round-trip through the reducer.

The deep review loop uses 9 state files under the resolved `{artifact_dir}/` owned by the target spec, and a run started with `--depth-mode=expansive` adds a tenth, `frontier-nodes.jsonl`:

| File | Format | Mutability |
|------|--------|------------|
| `deep-review-config.json` | JSON | Immutable after init, except the terminal `status: complete` flip and the `--extend=N` rewrite of `maxIterations`, `maxDurationMinutes`, `lineageMode` and `continuedFromRun` |
| `deep-review-state.jsonl` | JSON Lines | Append-only |
| `deep-review-findings-registry.json` | JSON | Auto-generated reducer state |
| `deep-review-strategy.md` | Markdown | Updated each iteration |
| `deep-review-dashboard.md` | Markdown | Auto-generated (read-only) |
| `.deep-review-pause` | Sentinel | Created/deleted by user |
| `resource-map.md` | Markdown | Emitted once at synthesis unless `resource_map.emit` is false |
| `review-report.md` | Markdown | Updated at synthesis |
| `iterations/iteration-NNN.md` | Markdown | Write-once |
| `frontier-nodes.jsonl` | JSON Lines | Append-only, expansive depth only (see Frontier Node Store) |
